          Try it at your mirror. If it’s not perfect, the courier takes it back immediately.
        </h2>
<p class="font-body-lg text-on-surface-variant">

        </p>

            </div>

</a>

<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">close</span>
</button>

  // Simple emergency scenario filter tab behavior
  document.querySelectorAll('.filter-tab').forEach(button => {
    button.addEventListener('click', () => {
      document.querySelectorAll('.filter-tab').forEach(b => {
        b.classList.remove('bg-inverse-surface', 'text-inverse-on-surface');
        b.classList.add('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-on-surface');
      });
      button.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-on-surface');
      button.classList.add('bg-inverse-surface', 'text-inverse-on-surface');
    });
  });

class="w-full pt-28 bg-surface"><div class="flex flex-col w-full">
<!-- Dynamic Catalog Sub-Header & Location Status -->
<section class="w-full bg-surface-container-lowest shadow-sm mb-space-lg">
<div class="max-w-[1280px] mx-auto px-margin-desktop py-space-md flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-space-md">
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1">
<nav class="flex items-center gap-space-xs font-label-md text-on-surface-variant">
<a class="hover:text-primary transition-colors" href="#">Catalog</a>
<span>/</span>
<span class="text-on-surface font-semibold">SoHo Direct Zone</span>
<span>/</span>
<span class="text-primary font-bold">All Ready-to-Wear</span>
</nav>
<div class="flex items-center gap-space-sm">
<h1 class="font-headline-md text-on-surface tracking-tight">148 Items Available for 30-Min Delivery in SoHo</h1>
<span class="bg-tertiary-container text-on-tertiary-container font-label-badge uppercase px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full flex items-center gap-1 shadow-sm">
<span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-tertiary-fixed animate-pulse"></span>
            Real-Time Boutique Stock
          </span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Quick Sorting / Density Bar -->
<div class="flex items-center gap-space-sm">
<div class="hidden sm:flex items-center bg-surface-container-low p-1 rounded-full">
<button class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full font-label-md text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-all" id="view-compact">Dense</button>
<button class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full font-label-md bg-surface-container-lowest text-on-surface shadow-sm transition-all" id="view-spacious">Editorial</button>
</div>
<div class="flex items-center gap-space-xs bg-surface-container-low px-space-md py-2 rounded-full">
<span class="font-label-md text-on-surface-variant">Sort by:</span>
<select class="bg-transparent font-label-md text-on-surface outline-none cursor-pointer">
<option>Fastest Delivery (Under 20m)</option>
<option>Closest Boutique Distance</option>
<option>Price: Low to High</option>
<option>Recently Restocked</option>
</select>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</section>
<!-- Main Content Layout -->
<div class="max-w-[1280px] w-full mx-auto px-margin-desktop pb-28">
<div class="flex flex-col lg:flex-row gap-gutter-desktop items-start relative">
<!-- LEFT FILTERS SIDEBAR (280px Desktop) -->

<!-- Filter Header & Reset -->

</div>

</div>
<!-- 1. Delivery Velocity Segment Slider -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-space-xs">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<span class="font-label-lg text-on-surface">Speed Guarantee</span>
<span class="font-label-badge text-tertiary-container bg-tertiary-fixed/30 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full" id="speed-indicator">≤ 25 mins</span>
</div>
<p class="font-body-sm text-on-surface-variant">Doorstep arrival from nearby storefronts</p>
<div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1.5 pt-2">
<button class="speed-pill py-1.5 rounded-full font-label-badge uppercase transition-all bg-surface-container-low text-on-surface-variant hover:bg-surface-container hover:text-on-surface" data-speed="15">&lt; 15m</button>
<button class="speed-pill py-1.5 rounded-full font-label-badge uppercase transition-all bg-inverse-surface text-inverse-on-surface font-bold shadow-sm" data-speed="25">&lt; 25m</button>
<button class="speed-pill py-1.5 rounded-full font-label-badge uppercase transition-all bg-surface-container-low text-on-surface-variant hover:bg-surface-container hover:text-on-surface" data-speed="35">&lt; 35m</button>
</div>
</div>
<!-- 2. Category Breakdown -->

</button>

</button>

</button>

</button>
<button class="category-filter flex items-center justify-between px-space-sm py-2 rounded-lg text-on-surface-variant hover:bg-surface-container-low hover:text-on-surface font-label-md transition-colors text-left" data-category="accessories">

<span class="font-label-badge text-on-surface-variant">29</span>
</button>
</div>

</label>
<label class="flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer p-2 rounded-lg bg-surface-container-low/50 hover:bg-surface-container-low transition-all">
<div class="flex items-center gap-2">

</label>
<label class="flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer p-2 rounded-lg bg-surface-container-low/50 hover:bg-surface-container-low transition-all">
<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
<input checked="" class="accent-primary rounded w-4 h-4 cursor-pointer" type="checkbox"/>

</div>

</label>

<div class="flex flex-col gap-space-xs pt-space-xs">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">

<div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 pt-1">

<div class="p-space-md flex flex-col flex-1 justify-between gap-space-sm">

<div class="flex items-center justify-between">

</div>

</div>
</div>
<!-- Active Try Bag Micro Tally & Trigger -->
<div class="flex items-center gap-space-sm shrink-0">
<div class="hidden md:flex flex-col text-right">
<span class="font-label-badge text-on-surface-variant uppercase">In Try Bag</span>
<span class="font-label-md text-on-surface font-bold" id="bag-count-text">2 items ($218 hold)</span>
</div>
<button class="bg-primary hover:bg-primary-container text-on-primary px-space-lg py-2.5 rounded-full font-label-md flex items-center gap-2 shadow-[0_8px_20px_-4px_rgba(255,62,108,0.35)] transition-all hover:scale-[1.02] active:scale-98">
<span>Review Try Bag</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">arrow_forward</span>
</button>
</div>
</aside>
<!-- Interactive Client Micro-Script -->
<script>
    // Size Filter Toggle
    document.querySelectorAll('.size-chip').forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const isSelected = btn.classList.contains('bg-inverse-surface');
        if (isSelected) {
          btn.classList.remove('bg-inverse-surface', 'text-inverse-on-surface', 'font-bold');
          btn.classList.add('bg-surface-container-low', 'text-on-surface');
        } else {
          btn.classList.add('bg-inverse-surface', 'text-inverse-on-surface', 'font-bold');
          btn.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-low', 'text-on-surface');
        }
      });
    });

    // Speed Pill Filter Toggle
    const speedIndicator = document.getElementById('speed-indicator');
    document.querySelectorAll('.speed-pill').forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        document.querySelectorAll('.speed-pill').forEach(b => {
          b.classList.remove('bg-inverse-surface', 'text-inverse-on-surface', 'font-bold', 'shadow-sm');
          b.classList.add('bg-surface-container-low', 'text-on-surface-variant');
        });
        btn.classList.add('bg-inverse-surface', 'text-inverse-on-surface', 'font-bold', 'shadow-sm');
        btn.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-low', 'text-on-surface-variant');
        
        const speed = btn.getAttribute('data-speed');
        if (speedIndicator) {
          speedIndicator.innerText = `\u2264 ${speed} mins`;
        }
      });
    });

    // Category Filter Selection
    document.querySelectorAll('.category-filter').forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        document.querySelectorAll('.category-filter').forEach(c => {
          c.classList.remove('bg-surface-container', 'text-on-surface');
          c.classList.add('text-on-surface-variant');
        });
        btn.classList.add('bg-surface-container', 'text-on-surface');
        btn.classList.remove('text-on-surface-variant');
      });
    });

    // Add to Try Button Toast Micro Interaction
    let tryCount = 2;
    const bagCountText = document.getElementById('bag-count-text');
    document.querySelectorAll('.add-try-btn').forEach(button => {
      button.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        tryCount++;
        if (bagCountText) {
          bagCountText.innerText = `${tryCount} items in Try Bag`;
        }
        const originalText = button.innerHTML;
        button.innerHTML = `<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">done</span> In Try Bag`;
        button.classList.add('bg-tertiary-container');
        button.classList.remove('bg-primary');
        setTimeout(() => {
          button.innerHTML = originalText;
          button.classList.remove('bg-tertiary-container');
          button.classList.add('bg-primary');
        }, 2000);
      });
    });

    // Reset Filters Action
    const resetBtn = document.getElementById('reset-filters');
    if (resetBtn) {
      resetBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        document.querySelectorAll('.size-chip').forEach(btn => {
          btn.classList.remove('bg-inverse-surface', 'text-inverse-on-surface', 'font-bold');
          btn.classList.add('bg-surface-container-low', 'text-on-surface');
        });
      });
    }
  </script>

<!-- Interactive Toast for Quick Doorstep Adding -->
<div class="fixed bottom-6 right-6 bg-inverse-surface text-inverse-on-surface px-5 py-3.5 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl flex items-center gap-3 transition-all duration-300 opacity-0 pointer-events-none translate-y-4 z-50" id="try-toast">
<div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-primary flex items-center justify-center text-on-primary">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">check</span>
</div>
<div class="flex flex-col">
<span class="font-label-md text-inverse-on-surface font-bold" id="toast-title">Added to Try Bag</span>
<span class="text-body-sm text-inverse-on-surface/70" id="toast-desc">Courier will bring this item in your selected size.</span>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<script>
  function handleTryAdd(btn, itemName, price) {
    const originalContent = btn.innerHTML;
    btn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]">check</span> Added to Try Bag';
    btn.classList.remove('bg-primary-container', 'text-on-primary-container');
    btn.classList.add('bg-tertiary', 'text-on-tertiary');
    
    // Update toast notification
    const toast = document.getElementById('try-toast');
    const toastTitle = document.getElementById('toast-title');
    const toastDesc = document.getElementById('toast-desc');
    
    toastTitle.textContent = itemName + ' (' + price + ')';
    toastDesc.textContent = 'Added to Doorstep Try-On Bag • Staged for dispatch';
    
    toast.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none', 'translate-y-4');
    toast.classList.add('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0');
    
    setTimeout(() => {
      toast.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none', 'translate-y-4');
      toast.classList.remove('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0');
      btn.innerHTML = originalContent;
      btn.classList.add('bg-primary-container', 'text-on-primary-container');
      btn.classList.remove('bg-tertiary', 'text-on-tertiary');
    }, 2800);
  }

  // Neighborhood filter pills interaction
  const filterPills = document.querySelectorAll('.filter-pill');
  filterPills.forEach(pill => {
    pill.addEventListener('click', function() {
      filterPills.forEach(p => {
        p.classList.remove('bg-on-surface', 'text-surface-container-lowest');
        p.classList.add('bg-surface-container', 'text-on-surface');
      });
      this.classList.remove('bg-surface-container', 'text-on-surface');
      this.classList.add('bg-on-surface', 'text-surface-container-lowest');
    });
  });

  // Search input live filter simulated highlight
  const searchInput = document.getElementById('boutique-search');
  searchInput.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
    const val = e.target.value.toLowerCase().trim();
    const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.boutique-card');
    cards.forEach(card => {
      const text = card.innerText.toLowerCase();
      if (!val || text.includes(val)) {
        card.style.display = 'block';
      } else {
        card.style.display = 'none';
      }
    });
  });
</script></main><footer class="w-full bg-surface-container-lowest mt-space-xl border-t-0 shadow-[0_-4px_24px_rgba(15,23,42,0.03)]"><div class="max-w-[1280px] mx-auto px-margin-desktop py-12"><div <!-- Interactive Logic -->
<script>
    (function() {
      // Category Pill Switching
      const pills = document.querySelectorAll('.cat-pill');
      pills.forEach(pill => {
        pill.addEventListener('click', () => {
          pills.forEach(p => {
            p.classList.remove('bg-inverse-surface', 'text-inverse-on-surface', 'shadow-sm');
            p.classList.add('bg-surface-container-low', 'text-on-surface-variant');
          });
          pill.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-low', 'text-on-surface-variant');
          pill.classList.add('bg-inverse-surface', 'text-inverse-on-surface', 'shadow-sm');
        });
      });

      // Try Bag Toast Interactivity
      const addButtons = document.querySelectorAll('.add-bag-btn');
      const toast = document.getElementById('tryToast');
      let timeout;

      addButtons.forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
          const originalText = btn.innerHTML;
          btn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px] animate-spin">sync</span><span>Added to Bag</span>';
          btn.classList.remove('bg-primary');
          btn.classList.add('bg-tertiary-container', 'text-on-tertiary-container');

          if (toast) {
            toast.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'translate-y-4', 'pointer-events-none');
            toast.classList.add('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0', 'pointer-events-auto');
            
            clearTimeout(timeout);
            timeout = setTimeout(() => {
              toast.classList.add('opacity-0', 'translate-y-4', 'pointer-events-none');
              toast.classList.remove('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0', 'pointer-events-auto');
              btn.innerHTML = originalText;
              btn.classList.add('bg-primary');
              btn.classList.remove('bg-tertiary-container', 'text-on-tertiary-container');
            }, 3500);
          }
        });
      });

      // Save store toggle
      const saveBtn = document.getElementById('saveStoreBtn');
      if (saveBtn) {
        saveBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const icon = saveBtn.querySelector('.material-symbols-outlined');
          const span = saveBtn.querySelector('span:last-child');
          if (icon.textContent === 'bookmark_border') {
            icon.textContent = 'bookmark';
            icon.classList.add('text-primary');
            span.textContent = 'Saved';
          } else {
            icon.textContent = 'bookmark_border';
            icon.classList.remove('text-primary');
            span.textContent = 'Save Store';
          }
        });
      }
    })();
  </script>

<!-- Client-side Interactive Countdown Script -->
<script>
    (function() {
      let seconds = 8 * 60 + 22; // 8 mins 22 secs
      const timerElement = document.getElementById('countdown-timer');
      
      if (timerElement) {
        const interval = setInterval(function() {
          if (seconds <= 0) {
            clearInterval(interval);
            timerElement.textContent = "00:00";
            return;
          }
          seconds--;
          const m = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
          const s = seconds % 60;
          timerElement.textContent = 
            (m < 10 ? '0' : '') + m + ':' + (s < 10 ? '0' : '') + s;
        }, 1000);
      }
    })();
  </script>
